@пл_різанина
I've lost count. Good thing I sit up high.
spatters on my feathers, and fewer and fewer pages left in the chronicle.
so many corpses that Munin will be jealous.
~1 a massacre without any plan. But effective.
~3 you're terrifying in a fight. Good thing you're on my side.
@пл_ледь
you staggered so hard I dug in my talons.
a little more and I'd be sitting on a tombstone instead of a shoulder.
~1 I've already started writing your obituary. A short one.
~1 if you fall, I'll fly over to someone smarter.
~2 careful. I've gotten used to you, strangely enough.
~3 don't scare me like that. Please.
@пл_бос

we did it. Well, you did, and I watched.
this goes into the chronicle in big runes.

~2 good work. Odin will hear about it from me first.
~3 I'm proud of you. I'll say it once, remember it.
@пл_дощ

rain. Wet feathers — the best reward for loyalty.
~1 a roof, for you, is apparently something theoretical.
~3 never mind, we'll wait it out together.

@спогад_вступ
From Hugin's chronicle, a page you'd rather tear out:
My chronicle says:
The skalds don't sing about this, so I will:
It came back to me, and, sadly, in detail:
For future generations who'll learn from your mistakes:
~1 Let me remind you, since your memory is a sieve:
~1 An instructive story. Instructive for someone who can learn:
~1 The record, chapter “Shameful”:
~2 Let's open the chronicle:
~2 One page from the past:
~3 Listen, friend, a story:
~3 While it's quiet, I'll recall something:
@спогад_хвіст
Written in runes, it won't rub off.
Odin read it and snorted.
The chronicle doesn't lie. Unlike you.
~1 You drew no conclusions, of course.
~1 Wisdom comes with age. It seems to have gotten lost on its way to you.
~1 Some lessons are learned slowly. Yours — never.
~2 Silly times. But ours.
~2 I wonder if you've gotten any smarter since. I doubt it.
~3 I'm glad I was there. Don't tell anyone.
~3 Things like that aren't forgotten. And shouldn't be.

# --- the raven in a stranger's hands (rev 11): only contempt
[stranger_talk] {чуж_вступ?} {чуж_мова} {чуж_кінець?}
[stranger_kill] {чуж_вступ?} {чуж_вбивство} {чуж_кінець?}

@чуж_вступ
Stranger,
{name},
Thief,
You who wear someone else's raven,
@чуж_мова
I'm not yours and never will be.
my master is {owner}. Remember that name, because I'll keep repeating it.
I see your every step and rejoice at none.
your thoughts don't interest me. Neither do you.
you smell of a stranger's hands.
I could help. But not you.
{owner} would do it better. Anything at all.
I sit quietly and count the days until I'm home.
even keeping quiet next to you is work.
I'm writing you into the chronicle. In the chapter “Thieves.”
don't talk to me. And don't look at me.
@чуж_кінець
Give me back.
And don't look at me like that.
Give me back to {owner}.
Leave me alone.
I'm waiting.
@чуж_вбивство
you killed it? So what, should I applaud?
my master would have done it cleaner.
one more corpse. Nothing for you, shame for me.
don't expect praise from me.
@чуж_смерть
You died, and I'm still not home.
The valkyries don't want you. Neither do I.
A pity. That you'll have to get up.
Maybe now you'll give me back?
@чуж_ледь
almost? Pity.
I won't help. Not you.
a little more and I'd have been free.
hold on. Not for your sake: I don't want to lie in a stranger's tombstone.
